DECIDED 10-08 (Fred) — the boundary rules (full text: service-layer design v17 §R7): GroupTrack owns the panels (moving GRP Awareness to the comm side rejected — "not an option"); the comm module has service-level access only, headless, returning results · requests GroupTrack → comm, events comm → GroupTrack, a sender and a matching function on each side · a request never waits for its outcome · recovery is a request, the action is performed in the comm module, reconnection is an event · errors are events with reason codes; GroupTrack owns the words ("your radio is not recovering — forget it and pair again") · native Meshtastic menus and screens stay UNCHANGED, opened by request OVER GroupTrack (how they END is open) · no new direct Meshtastic calls · one service at a time, both directions — slow and steady, using the existing where-used inventory (gen_xrefs_v2.sh) before and after each step · finish line = GroupTrack in its own module, the compiler finds zero.

Separate-app model and licensing (§R7.3; not legal advice): like ATAK + Meshtastic — a mesh app that owns the radio and gives service-level access; we can ship our own open-source (GPL) mesh app ("GroupTrack Comm") so riders need no stock Meshtastic; GroupTrack talks to it at arm's length. Today and through 2.7b everything is one APK = one GPL work (source public keeps it compliant). Confirmed: the boundary is in the right place and the handoffs are compatible with the licence terms. A comm screen opens ON TOP of GroupTrack (same task, live connection, not a new session) — it cannot be embedded inside a GroupTrack panel.

Measured: GroupTrack → Meshtastic now 9 files, ~106 lines, 52 service calls (was 19 files / ~139 refs this morning) — snapshot grouptrack_mesh_callsites_2026-10-08_v2.txt, grouped by API service.

1. Where we are — in one card

TESTERS Release v2.7a-internal-2026-10-07e — commit 3db12ebd9, version code 29321295 (2.7.14). Built, bundle-checked, archived, tested on Fred's tablet, uploaded to Play. The remote tester has picked it up.

ROOT CAUSE FOUND The tester's "compare never launches / back to the start" was not compare, the batch files or his tablet. He had switched all trail types off in the map key and dropped his trailhead on one of his tracks. GroupTrack converts tracks into trails where no trail exists, so it looked like a trail — but those trails were hidden by the key. By design (Fred 09-01, "we route what you put on the map") the AI search uses only the trails the map key shows → empty network → 0 routes → no compare header → nothing to compare. The search's "no connected network" exit was silent (log only) and the AI panel, given no results, reopens on its first page — so the rider just saw "back to the start".

IN FLIGHT Release …-07f (not built yet): removes the "Clear AI routes" buttons (wasted effort once the cause was found) and adds the real fix — TRAILHEADNEAR: the AI trailhead step refuses a trailhead with no shown trail within ½ mile, with a message, and the search's own reach becomes ½ mile. Decision owed: TRAILHEADNEAR v1 (one message) or v2 (two messages: "trail here but hidden by the map key" vs "no trail nearby") — see §5.

CODE Mac and GitHub at 3db12ebd9 (tag …-07e). The Mac's working tree may hold the uncommitted "release f step 1" if it was run — check first thing (§6, step 0).

OPEN GA (Thu/Fri) still waits on the overnight radio soak result (§7). TOPO+ / OpenTopoMap usage-policy question still open.

7. Radio — findings and open items

  • Reconnect loop (status: fixed): every ~76 s on old builds; gone with 30 s stationary (Droid 1 clean 20:53–21:34 on 10-06; Droid 2 clean all night with one self-healed drop).
  • Droid 1's radio …C4:6A dropped at the Bluetooth level (status 8) on 10-06 night despite 100 % battery → factory reset. After the reset it carried position.gps_en_gpio: 43 (the other radio …DE:DA has none; same firmware) → cleared to 0. Both radios then matched and their LED patterns synced. Cause of the drops still unproven.
  • "Every 4 s" TAK traffic was a counting artifact: each report received over the air is logged twice (uncompressed + compressed, 30 ms apart — two transmissions would need ~210 ms each). Own radio's reports appear once. Real rate ~8–12 s. to prove sender's numPacketsTx over 60 s (the CLI 2.7.11 --info did not print it).
  • Hop experiment: channel utilization ~6.6–7.0 % at hop 3 → ~5.0 % at hop 0 (relays ≈ 2 points with two radios); ~4–5 % is outside traffic on the frequency. Both radios now hop 2, 30 s / 100 m / 6 s, set by CLI (existing rides still carry hop 3 — a check-in to an old ride writes 3 back; use a fresh ride).
  • CLI over USB knocks the tablet's Bluetooth session off (one app connection at a time) — no CLI readings during a soak.
  • Soak: the hop-2 overnight run's logs (~/soak/droid1_night_2026-10-07_full.txt, droid2_… on the PC) — status not confirmed; compress and upload. GA decision depends on it. Fallback if not clean: ship GA with the 10-05 proven values (15 s, old smart, hop 3).
  • Debug Panel export writes a 0-byte file — defect, not urgent.

8. Tools and procedure changes today

Release procedure (standing, Mac): commit + tag → gt_release.sh <name> (refuses uncommitted code / untagged HEAD / missing keystore; checks the release certificate; bundle to the Desktop; nothing to Drive) → check_build_v2.sh ~/Desktop/<file>.aab (+ that release's markers) → build_publish_v2.sh --release <name> (one Drive copy, releases/ only; LATEST points at it) → PC: deploy by name + tablet test — the gate → Play upload from the Mac Desktop.

  • gt_release.sh (new), build_publish_v2.sh (new; a release publishes ONCE, was twice ≈ 210 MB), check_build_v2.sh (new; 15 markers incl. STATIONARY30 at 30 s; accepts an AAB path) — v1 retired. check_build_v1.sh had been written in another session 10-06 13:58.
  • Play Console uploads now happen on the Mac.
  • No tooling changes until the tablets can be loaded from the Mac (USB expansion) — then gt_build_v2. Idea recorded: Mac SMB share of build outputs so the PC installs without the Drive round-trip.
  • macOS showed a one-time Desktop permission prompt that paused a release copy — allow once.
  • Pasting very long commands sometimes splits in zsh (dquote>) — Ctrl+C and paste the shorter form.

9. Lessons (for the Living Master)

  • Ask what the rider actually did before building fixes. Ten rounds of compare/batch/leftover fixes were built before the cause (map key off + trailhead on a track) came from looking at his map. The first question for a single-device failure: version installed, and a screenshot of the screen.
  • Load and test locally before Play (Fred: "this is what happens when we do not load and test locally — a mess"). Now a gate in the procedure.
  • Fixes go where the rider is: a tool that needs leaving the screen destroys the state it should capture; a button in a dialog the flow never reaches does not exist.
  • Silent exits are bugs: two of the AI search's five zero-route exits only logged; and 0 routes reopens the AI panel at its first page. TRAILHEADNEAR stops the main cause up front; the silent restart itself remains (patch_aiempty was dropped by choice).
  • Tracks become trails where no trail exists — so a trailhead "on a track" is on a trail, and the map key can hide it.

a5a0c1768SVCPKG + RADIONAMES2THE "CONNECTING FOREVER" ROOT CAUSE: AndroidRadioControllerImpl.setDeviceAddress() restarted MeshService in the hard-coded package "com.geeksville.mesh" — since 07-26 the app id is com.grouptrack.android, so Android logged "Unable to start service … not found" at every connect/disconnect and the restart never happened; only a force stop recovered. Now context.packageName. Names: while the radio SERVICE says Connected (radioController.connectionState — no lag), the radio's own node list fills the table; GRP Awareness maps a scan entry "Meshtastic_deda" to its node id by the Bluetooth-name ending (= last 4 hex of the node id, e.g. !0ba3deda); every save logs the table and copies radio_names.json to /sdcard/Android/data/com.grouptrack.android/files/ (adb pull on a release build).

Failed-reconnect banner: "Your radio isn't answering. Open GRP Awareness, FORGET the radio, then PAIR it again. Not in the list? Turn it off and on."

Device-proven 10-04: silent/disconnected radio → my cart keeps moving (callsign), maps stay, Droid 2 keeps seeing Droid 1 (its radio broadcasts on its own); reconnect brings the group back; names: the table filled on Droid 2 from all three sources (beanie baby → Billy beans by config write; fred&June by TAK payload); after the bridge build names survive disconnects (first sight of a new name flips ~10 s after connect, then immediate).

Tester note sent with the build: "This update switches your radio's Bluetooth to No PIN the next time you check in or apply settings. If it then won't finish connecting: GRP Awareness → FORGET → PAIR → check in again. Once only."

RADIO LESSONS 10-04 (these radios can look healthy while out of step): (1) service started under the old package → stuck connecting (fixed); (2) Android's pairing out of step after a config write → link up, NO DATA, power cycle useless, FORGET + re-PAIR fixed it; (3) a radio's own state wrong → one-way (it heard others, nobody heard it) → a radio reset fixed it, then re-apply config. Bluetooth name = "Meshtastic_" + last 2 bytes of the Bluetooth address; node id = last 4 bytes of the address (DB:92:0B:A3:DE:DA = !0ba3deda = Meshtastic_deda). Droid 1's plain logcat dump shows only the SYSTEM buffer (no app lines) — use logcat -b main, or the pulled radio_names.json.

🔴 10-03 — FIELD DAY: RADIO LINKS DROPPED — the 2.7b reconnect rule (read first)

⭐⭐⭐ 2.7b — FIRST ITEMS FROM 10-03 (testers' field day + tonight's release checks). FIELD-2026-10-03

What happened (Fred): testers on the OLD software had a bad day — T1000-E Bluetooth links went quiet while radios sat still; Android still showed "connected"; tablets without GPS had no position; the map went blank; RECORD recorded nothing. 2.7a already fixes the position half: when the radio fails, the tablet's own GPS replaces the last known position. Not fixed: while the radio is silent the tablet cannot see the other carts — that is a RECONNECT problem.

Research (10-03, cited in chat): Meshtastic documents that TRACKER/SENSOR with power saving sleep and shut Bluetooth down between broadcasts (nRF52 included — the T1000-E is nRF52). The Android app is known to show "connected but sleeping" while actually disconnected, and not to reconnect by itself. The fork ALREADY sends a ToRadio heartbeat every 30 s (AndroidRadioInterfaceService.kt:154–169, 299–311) — so keep-alive alone did not prevent the drops. TAK_TRACKER stays the default (chosen 09-25: radios broadcast their own TAK position with callsign) — not changed without the bench test. Fred: "cannot reboot what you are not connected to" — a remote restart cannot rescue a dead link.

THE RULE (Fred 10-03):

  1. No radio talking → Android mode: position from the tablet's GPS, map and route as normal; the group is not shown as if live.
  2. GRP Awareness turns RED while no radio is connected.
  3. GroupTrack cycles disconnect → reconnect automatically (the fallback to tablet GPS IS the detection), repeating until the radio answers; after a minute or two without success: "Turn your radio off and on."
  4. On reconnect, my cart is matched by CALLSIGN (not the radio's device id); GRP Awareness back to connected; other carts reappear. The group survives: no carts dropped, no roles reset (the 09-29 group rule).
  5. Uses the ONE reconnect (inventory found three copies — 2.8 service A6); answers passed/failed with a reason (R3).
  6. No-GPS tablets: hold the last known position with "Radio not answering — position from hh:mm".

BENCH TEST first (two T1000-Es on 2.7a, still for 20 min): Radio A TAK_TRACKER (as shipped) vs Radio B TAK — does the Bluetooth link survive; does the other tablet still receive positions? Decides whether the default config changes. Also confirm 2.7a's power-saving setting (grep power/saving in grouptrack_default.json).

⭐⭐⭐ 10-03 — AGREEMENTS CAPTURED (read first)

⭐⭐⭐ 2.8 — AGREEMENTS ON HOW TO PROCEED (Fred 10-03).

  1. Renumbered: the comm-layer separation is release 2.8 (a major revision), not 2.7b. Two models: 2.7.x stays on the Windows PC (current branch, fixes only); 2.8 starts on the Mac Air from a pull. Git: tag the released 2.7a commit v2.7a (once Saturday's AAB is chosen) → branch 2.8 from the tag → the Mac checks out 2.8; 2.7 fixes are carried forward into 2.8, never back; same app id and Play listing.
  2. The rules: open payloads, private processes; the three separation tests on every service (R1 only door · R2 plain data, no shared state · R3 expect failure); ⛔ no convergence fixes; Android stays on its proven Meshtastic code.
  3. The Group riding switch: Settings On/Off. On = GRP Awareness available, radio connection required, comm layer started; Off = solo. The install question sets the first value and runs or skips the initial radio default config as now. The value lives in the default config API as a device setting — "groupRiding": { "enabled", "method", "setBy", "setAtMs" } — read at every session start; never in a ride package.
  4. No tick: the comm layer hands off deduped, normalised messages; GroupTrack processes each on arrival; time-based status is derived; one next-deadline timer for silence. Ends the 3-second cycle that could show 6-second-old values.
  5. CoT is the data standard: Layer 1 and the panels use a CoT-shaped model (uid, type, time/start/stale, point with accuracy, contact, group + role, track, status); status follows each sender's stale time; mesh = Meshtastic's compact TAK packet, IP = TAK protocol, converted in the comm layer; GroupTrack's ride / check-in / END in a CoT detail extension; roles LEADER→Team Lead, MIDDLE→RTO, TAIL_GUNNER→Forward Observer, RIDER→HQ.
  6. The API, first pass: GroupTrack Comm API v0.2 (GroupTrackComm_API_DRAFT_2026-10-03_v2.kt, module :grouptrack-comm): CommLink (A1–A6), CommIdentity (B7), CommConfig (C8–C11), CommMessaging (D12–D14, the event stream), CommSession (E15–E16), GroupCommPlugin (F17 + start/stop from the setup value), CommSetupStore, GroupState (apply / status / nextDeadline). Each API definition = model name · module name · contract data in · service performed · data returned · the Android functions moved into the plugin · the Meshtastic open-source code they use.
  7. The inventory (31 functions, 11 files): reconnect ×3 → one; reporting frequency ×2 → one; the TAK send reads check-in state → passed in; installProfile fire-and-forget → returns a result; configurator + compare are pure → shareable with the iPad; five per-setting editors + the older profile builders → reachability check; TakRoleStore (18 lines, Meshtastic core) → service-owned.
  8. The order: any critical 2.7a fixes first → the 2.8 design phase (finish the API definitions; the Apple column to functional calls, cited; the contract tests; the CoT research) → the Mac environment → build 2.8 Android → start Apple on the iPad (spikes; the config go/no-go before plugin code).

Full design: grouptrack_service_layer_design_2026-10-03_v8.html (32 sections); its content is carried here and in the service-layer card below.

FOUND AND FIXED — THE STRANDED IMPORT

Why imports "never finished and could not be restarted — we had to clear data": extract and import ran as WorkManager jobs enqueued with ExistingWorkPolicy.KEEP; the coordinator waited on them with no time limit. A job stuck queued held it forever → isImporting stayed true → the picker silently ignored Load ("import already running — ignoring request"). Closing the app killed the coordinator but not the job (Android keeps its own job database); KEEP re-attached the next import to the dead job; only clearing data removed it. Also: the gate shows the picker only on an empty trail database, so a part-finished import opened the map with half a state. Fixed by INPROCESS + KILLEDIMPORT (ec749561c), verified on Droid 2.

REJECTED — AND WHY

  • KILLEDIMPORT v2 — cancel leftover jobs at startup + a 3-minute queued-job limit: managed the background jobs instead of removing them; Fred's in-process call replaced it before it ran.
  • The queue-only legibility patch (white + 11sp on two lines, dimText to grey): Fred — "just the dark slate", then white app-wide; the search found 162 uses on 47 screens.
  • Release-notes entries for the import and slate fixes: never seen in the field.
  • Landscape promo panels; two separate promo sets: Fred — portrait, one cohesive weekly series.
  • The obfuscation change in the 10-02 upload: renaming breaks by-name code only in release builds; it needs its own tested build (2.7b).

RULES LEARNED

  • A long job with two owners strands people. One owner, visible start to finish, that dies with the app, is the safe shape for anything a rider waits on.
  • Read the colour, not the hex: a "dim" token can be a whole app's secondary text — search every use before changing one.
  • Context-based classification needs a human pass: the slate patch called a field label "disabled" because disabledTextColor sat two lines above — the report caught it.
  • Play links: keep &hl=en_US; the bare link can 404 depending on region.

DECIDED 10-01 (Fred)

  • The radio update is 2.7a — 2.7a moves GroupTrack to TAK messaging, so every radio needs the GroupTrack default (grouptrack_default.json, the config tested all along). The app cannot tell whether a rider owns a radio, so it ASKS — once, at first start, after background location and before the state-imports test (the trail check runs every launch: evaluateState → needsTrailData).
  • YES → GRP Awareness (a Main.kt overlay, drawn above the NavHost, so above the gate): connect → SELECT A RIDE AND APPLY → GroupTrack default → apply. NO → later, in GRP Awareness, before the first ride. Pairing code line: 123456 is the default; a radio with a screen may show another. Callsign lines: your callsign becomes the radio's name; change it on each additional radio.
  • Not asked again after NO, after a verified apply (all 19 checks), or when a 2.7 saved config exists (a .cfg WITH its .json companion in convoy_backups/<radio>/ — old _pre_master.cfg and V2 json-only files do not count); answered-this-session flag because the gate re-evaluates on every resume.
  • No image save, no per-radio tag. The standard apply already saves the radio "as found" before its first write; the compare in Saved configs shows the initial differences. (Withdrawn the same day: a Meshtastic image to Downloads; a per-radio done tag.)
  • The connect panel is GRP Awareness — it pairs as well as reconnects (Fred forgot a device and re-paired through it).
  • Route arrows on both maps — cosmetic, the track-arrow model; the ride-map freeze does not apply to it.
  • ⭐⭐ CREATE A RIDE IS THE 2.7a HEADLINE. Route creation was 2.6's story. Rides are created ONLY by M — tapping a route or a track on the Ride Planning Map (planner route → description → CREATE RIDE; drawn route → details → ADD A RIDE; track → details → CREATE RIDE → trailhead → CREATE ROUTE & RIDE). Map Features → SELECT only finds the feature. The Ride Map has no ride creation.
  • ⭐⭐ THE COMMUNITY MESSAGE (Fred): Riding together starts with a ride. The ride package — a route at its defined trailhead, on a set date and time, with the maps it needs and its radio configuration — sent as an invite. One click, everyone has the same preparation; check-in applies the radio configuration for them. No more night before spent downloading an area and hoping the trail goes where you think it does. That is how the community grows.
  • ⭐⭐ THE VOCABULARY (Fred) — never mix them: a ride is the package (a bundle of joy); a route is the path the ride requires; a track is a recording; a trail is on the ground; a waypoint is a marked place; a trailhead is where a route starts and riders meet. You go on a ride, follow its route, and record your track. The planner and Route+ build ROUTES; Create a Ride builds the ride package.
  • The Play Store link goes in the ride invite — which link (store listing vs tester opt-in) still to choose.
  • ⭐ A failed state import never strands the rider: remove the failed manifest, say "The failed state import was removed. Select a state and process again", return to the state picker. No resume for now. The import cannot be a background job — it runs too long; the rider must see it end.
  • Maps: the time estimate is good; zoom by map type — satellite to z18 (meaningful detail), topo stops at z16 (a drawn map is empty deeper). Corridors are more efficient and guarantee map coverage along the trail at any zoom — you will always see the trail; areas are for exploring.
  • Queue screen: jobs-in-queue and the estimated completion line in bright white (light blue on black is unreadable).
  • Captures: the empty-folder tree (v4 on G:), Map touches on both maps, shared images placed by Claude; the old Meshtastic menu screens left off; END is the "Save your ride" survey.

FOUND AND FIXED — THE SLASH

Planner SHARE / EXPORT "failed" with the track's old text name in a full path — the ride map worked. Hours went to ids, panels, a timing race and lookup fallbacks (a buildTrackGpxById resolve patch was written and withdrawn). The cause was in the one function not yet read: ConvoyTrackOps.shareGpx / exportGpxToDownloads name the file <name>.gpx cleaning only spaces; the track's name held a / (a date), so the write threw and "Share failed: <path>" showed the exception. Fred's tracks on the ride map simply had no slash. Fixed: safe file names (GPXNAME, 8009a52f6), verified on Droid 1. Fred: "of the 90 tracks on my device I happened to pick a track name with a slash."

REJECTED — AND WHY

  • A Meshtastic image of the radio saved to Downloads before the update — Fred: skip it, keep it simple; the first apply already keeps the as-found values, and the compare shows them.
  • A per-radio "done" tag, or reading "done" from the radio's settings — a ride apply changes channel and preset, so a settings check would offer to overwrite the rider's ride config. One stored answer instead.
  • Detecting a radio from the node list or myNodeInfo at startup — the gate has only a Context, and before connection "no radio" and "not connected yet" look the same. Ask instead.
  • The buildTrackGpxById resolve-by-name patch (trackshare v1/v2) — aimed at an unproven cause; the real cause was the file name. Withdrawn before it ran.
  • Create a Ride filed under Map Features (v43) — rides are created by M, tapping on the planning map; Map Features only finds the feature (v44).
  • Resuming a failed state import at startup — the original design; too much for now. Remove, tell, re-pick.

RULES LEARNED 10-01

  • Read the function that writes the file before theorising about how the file was chosen. The slash would have shown in the first look at shareGpx.
  • The post-install check is stale: since 2.7 the databases are app-private (GroupTrackStorage) — ls /sdcard/Documents/GroupTrack/*.db finds nothing on a healthy install. Check on screen: starts normally, trails on the map, tracks in Map Features.
  • head hides results: a grep … | head -10 stopped inside convoy/ and hid Meshtastic's own export in feature/settings.
  • An asset copied in mid-build is not in that build. All six counts read 0 inside the APK/AAB; the rebuild carried them. Always read the docs inside the artifact.
  • A once-only startup prompt cannot be captured on a tablet that already answered — use the sacrificial tablet: adb -s 24039703201775 shell pm clear com.grouptrack.android (also the only way to clear an app-private import manifest today).
  • Capture the planning map with Hide Mesh — 21 captures needed the Meshtastic rail cropped off.
  • tar restores folder dates from the archive — new folders do not look new.

DECIDED TODAY (Fred)

  • Carts: REMOVE / ACTIVATE are actions on each other cart (my own has none); a removal stays until ACTIVATE or END; END resets every cart to Active, ride or no ride; each tablet its own list. Certified on tablets.

🔴 PROBLEM 1 — MY RADIO'S OWN REPORTS NEVER REACH MY TABLET (first)

Symptom: GPS panel → APPLY → Written · Disconnecting in 3 s · Reconnecting in 20 s · Done. GRP Awareness goes RED (a real disconnect) then GREEN and flashing (reconnected, hearing the group). SELECT CART lists BOTH carts, but my cart's pointer is not on the map; a manual DISCONNECT then CONNECT in GRP Awareness restores it. Also: "no activity for 24 minutes" in the app while the Mesh Nodes screen shows the radio every 15 s; the radio broadcasts every 10 s and the other tablet sees it.

Analysis: a radio's broadcasts are never received back by its own tablet (TAK reports included), so my radio's record on my tablet (position, last heard) is refreshed only by the radio's own local updates. The GPS panel's reconnect is a shortcut — ConvoyViewModel.radioOps(ui): disconnect() = ui.setDeviceAddress("n"), reconnect() = ui.setDeviceAddress(saved) — which appears to reset that record; the position comes back empty, the list shows the cart, the map cannot draw it. GRP Awareness's buttons use Meshtastic's connection manager (scanModel.disconnect() / connect) and keep it. The stale last-heard may also make my cart count as silent/Lost.

Fix, two parts: (a) the GPS panel AND the check-in's radio setup (RadioConfigWriter uses the same ops) disconnect/reconnect exactly as GRP Awareness does (Main.kt: onDisconnect = { scanModel.disconnect() }; find the CONNECT path) — the check-in's 09-28 first-run blindness looked the same; (b) my cart's position and presence from the tablet (GPS; the check-in) — the identity rule. ⚠ (b) changes how MY CART is built: design with Fred before any code. Test: APPLY → my pointer stays, not counted Lost; the check-in's setup the same.

🔴 PROBLEM 2 — THE SURVEY OWNED BY THE ROUTE

Decision (Fred): "the survey belongs to the route" — "route is the key"; ten riders on one ride = one route's rating; even if tied to a ride, it resolves to the route; no track reference (one track per ride kept — the first replicated). Today: END → EndOfRideForm → pendingSurvey → finalizeTrack() → ConvoyRideStore.saveSurvey(trackId, sv) (keyed to the TRACK; asked only on a public scheduled ride). At END the ride is known (endingCheckIn) and rideForEdit(rideId).routeId gives the route. Needed: the route's GLOBAL identity (same on every tablet, carried in the ride file); the survey table keyed by route (ride as context) + a migration; saveSurvey by route. Read first: ConvoyRideStore.kt (RideSurveyInput, saveSurvey, the table), the route table, the ride file format. 3.0: the device is the target; routes → rides → surveys are the first data mined for AWS.

🔴 PROBLEM 3 — WAYPOINTS IN THE ARTIFACT DETAIL PANEL

Tap a waypoint → the existing artifact detail panel → rename · reclassify (type) · delete. No other way to clean up waypoints today. Read first: ArtifactDetailPanel and its callers, the waypoint table/types, the map's tap handling (JS + Kotlin bridge; the checklist's 00-TAP section).

⭐⭐ THE ROLE ANSWER — proven 09-28 evening. Ride roles travel in a standard TAK position report that the TABLET fills in; every tablet lifts the role from the report received (Fred's idea). No radio settings, no firmware, no reboot, no custom messaging. Leader → TeamLead · Middle → RTO · Tail gunner → ForwardObserver · (HQ = Rider, for a reassignment). The radios' own TeamMember reports are ignored, so they cannot erase a role. Only the three special roles broadcast. The node array is the single source (my own role goes into the same store); the tick assigns lead and tail from the roles; the HUD, markers and labels follow within a couple of seconds. REC's lead question appears only while no lead is locked.

What failed first (do not re-try): the role in the radio's own reports via Meshtastic's TAK Identity (ModuleConfig.tak) — the T1000-Es ignore it on their firmware, even after a power cycle (2.8-era feature; Fred will not flash 2.8); a custom PRIVATE_APP frame and request/reply schemes — rejected as custom messaging; roles through the configurator's apply — a settings write per change. Full record: living checklist v98+, THE RADIO → TICK HANDOFF … UPDATED 09-28.

Agreed rules for the remaining role work: broadcast every 30 s from check-in until 3 minutes after REC, then silent; roles fixed until a reassignment (which broadcasts the same way) or END; blank role = Rider; everybody checks in first thing at the trailhead (the check-in puts each radio on the ride network — until then it hears nothing), special roles among the first; the ride starts when everyone has checked in. Measure: three timestamps (sent · received · applied), absolute ms.

Track design notes (lead track vs my track — goals, options, January field-test questions): GroupTrack_TrackDesign_LeadTrack_MyTrack_2026-09-28_v1.html, and in the checklist's 08b CONVOY TRACKING REWRITE · UPDATED 09-28.

2 · THE ROLES DAY (its own day; nothing else touched):

  • Every cart has a role — Leader, Middle, Tail gunner or Rider — chosen at check-in and carried in its reports with its callsign (the check-in writes the role to the radio: TAK module config). One Leader, one Middle, one Tail gunner per ride; everyone else is a Rider.
  • The lead and tail ASSIGNMENTS are REMOVED (the REC lead-cart popup and SET AS LEAD): the tick READS the roles from the reports, the same on every device. The map shows callsign · role for every cart.
  • SELECT CART (the button beside MY CART) — available to every cart: the list of all carts on the ride (callsign + role); tap one to open its display.
  • From that list, the Leader, Middle or Tail gunner (only they) can SWITCH ROLES with the selected cart: pick which special role it takes → the selected cart gets that role, that role's previous holder takes the selected cart's old role (a swap — never two holders). Any special-role holder can move ANY of the three — so a broken-down Leader never leaves the ride stuck. Every switch announced on every tablet; the tick follows the latest switch, not a silent radio's old role. (REMOVE FROM RIDE: restricted the same way.)
  • The check-in preloads the ride map ("Show this ride's route and trailhead on the ride map", ticked by default, rides only): CHECK IN clears the ride map to the ride's route and trailhead, fitted (through the ride map's own saved state); at REC the leader's track and the group's positions appear on top.

3 · 🔴 Blocking riders — fix before anything else

IMPORTLOCK — the importer can be bricked, and a rider cannot clear it. ⭐ Root cause ISOLATED 09-20: it is beginImport()'s IN-MEMORY LATCH at HomeStatePickerScreen:133, NOT the stranded manifest. A dead run leaves the latch set and nothing launches — no message, no log. Force-stop alone frees it. Fix is 2.6g, two parts: release the latch, then SRCLIST — list the state's sources, tick what is already held (bypassed), untick to pull one back in. ⭐ SRCLIST subsumes the resume prompt entirely: a failed source simply arrives unticked, so "resume" is pressing import again. ⚠ Make the tick an ACTION, not a status — the maps checkbox had exactly that ambiguity and it cost a day.

Why it pays: the Utah rerun fetched 49,125 records to insert 52, for 27–31 minutes. A rerun pays the full cost for almost none of the benefit — and area downloads over a held state have the same problem.

4 · Releases in flight

2.6g — IMPORTLOCK latch + SRCLIST (above). The short list for today's riders.

2.6h — 50-state PUBLIC/PRIVATE trail compliance: default all trails public, private from Geofabrik PBF access tags, ownership polygons. See the 2.6h section in the master.

2.7 — the mesh/GroupTrack separation. Today built the identity chain and the ride shell; tomorrow's eight items are in the master's In-Progress. Config first (built for TAK), then TAK; hard cut to a new base config; T1000-E pinned at firmware 2.6.11.60ec05e, no firmware updates.

3.0 — server side. Parking lot: the ride sync queue · choosing other people's organizers and orgs · premium gating at RUNTIME, never by version code · AWS Phase B · what happens to downloaded community routes when premium lapses.

7 · Rules a new conversation must not break

Added 09-25:

  • (09-29) No fundamental change to a working function without a conversation with Fred first.
  • (09-29) Never fall back on the last-known position map; the previous tick is the last known position.
  • (09-29) Every cart speaks only for itself — no reports about other carts; the Leader is the last assignment.
  • (09-29) The method: a collection of related fixes → Kotlin compile → commit → release build → install. Present every file for download.
  • (09-28) Ride roles travel ONLY in the tablet-filled TAK position report; no radio settings writes for roles, no custom messaging (Fred). The radios' own reports are never used to set a role.
  • (09-28) A radio joins the ride network only at its check-in — everybody checks in first thing at the trailhead.
  • (09-28) Before any calculation change: a restore-point tag, pushed. One build and one test per change.
  • Radios are configured with the configurator — Android's settings screens cannot express seconds (hour presets only); hand-set values caused the 3 s rhythm.
  • One staleness rule: the Settings slider, applied AT THE READ. Never re-add a second time filter downstream.
  • No whole-image restore, anywhere. Apply = managed fields only.
  • Until the callsign override exists, configure only your own radio (the configurator writes the runner's callsign).
  • TAKDUMP is a diagnostic — remove before the 2.7 release.
  • ⭐ 09-23: every apply starts from the ride JSON — on every device, whatever the target. The JSON is a stored file written on every Save; Send attaches it; import stores a received one unchanged.
  • ⭐ 09-23: a ride is in progress until distributed, then ONLY the date changes. State is derived from distributed_at. Complete = route + trailhead + network.
  • ⭐ 09-23: every route has a trailhead in its RECIPE (anchor). Route+ first point needs one within ½ mile; ride creation needs one; track conversion WILL need one (task in the master).
  • ⭐ 09-23: nothing filters nodes by channel NAME — the channel KEY does, at the radio. Prefix is Group+6; GroupTrack is the reserved default channel.
  • ⛔ The convoy map is FROZEN — ConvoyScreen.kt is not edited. The MENU (ConvoySubMenu from ui/Main.kt) and the planner are not frozen.
  • Leader = organizer = a role on the profile. Several orgs per rider. In 2.7 the leader of a ride you create is ALWAYS you.
  • Networks generated in exactly three places — org added · leader chooses "my own" · a unique ride network. GT id + 32-byte key + base values, TX at or below the licence-free limit. Immutable; rotation makes a new one. ALWAYS write both mesh and nucleus blocks.
  • ONE WRITE PATH — profile maintenance creates every rider, org, leader setting and network.
  • Other people exist only as ride data. Callsigns may repeat; riders matched by DEVICE (tak_uid, or node_num).
  • Four role rules — single holder · release then claim · only a person changes a role (a quiet radio means nothing) · leaving releases it.
  • Only ROUTES travel, distilled to a polyline. Identity is (type, geom_hash), never the hash alone.
  • Nothing written until Save. No ghosts.
  • Nothing is sent to GroupTrack unless the rider shares — and sharing sends only their ride and their email.
  • ⛔ No OSM-derived data on GroupTrack infrastructure, ever. ⚠ This may exclude the route RECIPE from ride files — check what readRouteNotes actually holds before building the writer.
  • CODE RULES 1–3 and the GOLDEN RULE (never reuse a filename) — see the master.
